If g_center positive for high update? We want minimize, so subtract: p_new ∝ p exp(-η g). If g has high where gradient high. Centering: p_i exp(-η(g_i-mean)) same. Good. Let's formalize `objective_B(p)` function:
```
def conv(p):
    n=len(p); L=2*n-1; fft_len=1<<(L-1).bit_length()
    P=np.fft.rfft(p, fft_len)
    B=np.fft.irfft(P*P, fft_len)[:L]
    return B
```
Need ensure p sum1 and nonnegative. Use float64.
`grad_from_weights(p,w)`:
```

w_rev = w[::-1]
fft_len=1<<((L+n-1)-1).bit_length() # >= L+n-1 =3n-2
W=np.fft.rfft(w_rev, fft_len)
P=np.fft.rfft(p, fft_len)
conv=np.fft.irfft(W*P, fft_len)[:L+n-1]
c=conv[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
# due numerics, clip
return c
```

Detailed update:
```
while time.monotonic() < deadline:
    B=conv(p); M=B.max()
    if M < best_M: update
    if M <= target_C* (1+1e-12): break? target_C=1.5027/(2n)
    # weights

    # If M tiny? set shift
    z = cpar * (B - M) / max(M, 1e-300)  # ≤0, max 0
    # clip z to [-700,0] to avoid underflow but not needed
    z = np.clip(z, -700, 0)
    expz = np.exp(z)
    wsum = expz.sum()
    if wsum <= 0 or not finite: w = zeros; w[argmax]=1
    else w=expz/wsum
    # To avoid all mass on unique max if B ties? If unique, gradient may be constant and stuck? Actually if p not symmetric, shifted p not constant; if p uniform and max central stuck. But high cpar okay. If stuck, lower cpar or random.
    cgrad = grad_from_weights(p,w)
    g = 2*cgrad
    # standardize
    gmin,gmax = g.min(), g.max()
    if gmax <= gmin + tiny: # no movement, perturb
       p = add smooth noise; continue
    u = (g - (gmin+gmax)/2) / ((gmax-gmin)/2) # range -1,1
    # optional top active only: If cpar too high, w degenerate; we can blend weights with top K? Softmax handles.
    # line search over L (max log change)

       # But if p has zeros, stays zero; we maintain floor.
       s = p_new.sum()

       p_new = np.maximum(p_new, 0)

```

Let's set constants:
- `target_score=1.50274349`
- `target_M = target_score/(2*n)` for current n.

Optimize loop pseudo:
```
def optimize_from(p0, time_limit, seed, label, initial_M):
  p = normalize_pos(p0)
  B,M = convB(p)
  best_p = p.copy(); best_M=M; best_score=2*n*M
  current_M=M
  no_best=0
  base_bad=0.02
  it=0
  stoch_frac=0.6
  K=...
  c_par=5.0
  while time<limit:
    it+=1
    # maybe if M <= target_M*(1+1e-12): break? but keep best
    # update best
    if M < best_M: ... no_best=0
    if best_score <= target_score*(1-1e-12): maybe stop large
    # compute top
    Kcur = choose_K(B,M)
    top_idx = argpartition...
    top_vals=B[top_idx]
    # sample/use weights
    if rng.random()<stoch_frac and Kcur>1:
       # sample k
       w = np.exp(c_par*(top_vals - top_vals.max())/max(top_vals.max(),eps))
       if sum>0: w/=w.sum else uniform
       k = int(rng.choice(top_idx, p=w))
       g = active_gradient_single(p,k)
    else:
       wfull = zeros; wfull[top_idx]=w (same)
       cvec = grad_fft(p,wfull)
       g = 2*cvec
    # standardize
    gmax=np.max(g); gmin=np.min(g)
    if gmax <= gmin + 1e-18:
       # perturb current
       p=perturb(p,0.1); B,M=conv(p); continue
    u=(g-gmin)/(gmax-gmin)  # [0,1]
    # Choose L values
    Lmax = 1.0 + 0.2*min(10, no_best/100)? Maybe increase when stuck.
    Ls = [Lmax, Lmax*0.5, Lmax*0.25, Lmax*0.125]
    logp=np.log(p) # p>0
    logp_shifted = None
    best_cand=None; best_cand_M=M; accepted=False
    for Lam in Ls:
       lp=logp - Lam*u
       lp -= np.max(lp)
       p_new=np.exp(lp)
       s=p_new.sum()
       if s<=0: continue
       p_new/=s
       # maybe floor
       p_new=np.maximum(p_new, floor)
       s2=p_new.sum()
       p_new/=s2
       Bnew,Mnew=convB(p_new)
       if Mnew < current_M - 1e-16:
          accepted=True; break
       elif Mnew < best_cand_M:
          best_cand=p_new; best_cand_M=Mnew
    if accepted:
       current_M=Mnew
    else:
       # Accept best_cand if not too worse or with annealing
       bad_allow = current_M * (base_bad + 0.05*min(1, no_best/1000))
       if best_cand_M < current_M + max(bad_allow, 1e-18):
           current_M=best_cand_M; p=best_cand
       else:
           # reject all: maybe tiny step or perturb
           # choose smallest Lam candidate perhaps slightly worse; use it if no_best high?
           if no_best > 200:
              current_M=best_cand_M; p=best_cand
           else:
              # no state change, maybe try different k next iteration; count? 
              pass
    p = np.maximum(p, floor); p/=p.sum()
    B,M=convB(p) # we already have Bnew maybe; avoid extra
    if M < best_M: ... else no_best+=1
    if no_best > 1000:
       # reset to best with perturbation
       p = perturb(best_p, sigma=0.1)
       B,M=conv(p)
       no_best=0
    # update c_par, K, stoch_frac maybe
```
This does convB twice if accepted? We compute Bnew inside; can set B,M=Bnew. If rejected and no state change, B remains. If reset, convB.

```
def perturb(p, alpha=0.2):
   # add smooth random log noise
   theta = rng.normal(0, alpha, n)
   # low-pass: maybe FFT filter to reduce high frequency
   th = np.fft.rfft(theta); mask = np.ones(len(th)); mask[len(th)//3:]=0; theta=np.fft.irfft(th, n)
   p_new = p * np.exp(theta - np.mean(theta))
   p_new = np.maximum(p_new, floor); normalize
```

```
def choose_top(B, M, base_frac=0.02, maxK=1024, minK=32):
   L=len(B)
   width = max(M*0.05, 1e-12)  # relative
   count = int(np.sum(B >= M - width))
   K = max(minK, min(L, count, maxK))
   # Ensure if count < minK use minK
   if K >= L: top=np.arange(L)
   else: top=np.argpartition(B, L-K)[-K:]
```

Generate initial p candidates:
```
def make_smooth_random(n, rng, scale=1.0):
   # theta = random Fourier low-pass
   theta = rng.standard_normal(n)
   th = np.fft.rfft(theta)
   m = th.size
   mask = np.zeros(m); # low frequencies
   cutoff = max(2, int(0.2*m))
   mask[:cutoff]=1
   th *= mask
   theta = np.fft.irfft(th, n)
   theta -= theta.mean()
   p = np.exp(scale*theta)
   p = np.maximum(p, floor); normalize
```

```
    g[lo:hi+1] = 2.0 * p[k-hi : k-lo+1][::-1]
```

Now, `normalize_pos(p, floor=1e-15)`:
```
p = np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
p = np.clip(p,0.0,1000.0)
s = p.sum()
if s < 1e-9: p = np.ones(n)/n
else: p = p/s
p = np.maximum(p, floor)
p = p/p.sum()
return p
```

Perturb:
```
def perturb(p, alpha, rng):
    n=p.size
    theta = rng.standard_normal(n)
    # low pass 50%
    th=np.fft.rfft(theta)
    m=th.size
    cut=max(2, m//4)
    th[cut:]=0.0
    theta=np.fft.irfft(th, n)
    theta -= theta.mean()
    pnew = p * np.exp(alpha*theta)
    return normalize_pos(pnew)
```
For alpha up to 1. Could also random permutation? Not.

Maybe add antisymmetric perturbation: `p_new = p * exp(alpha*(sin(2πr i/n)))`. This specifically breaks symmetry. We can generate a few:
```
def trig_perturb(p, amp, freq): theta=amp*np.sin(2πfreq*i/n + phase); p*exp(theta)
```

Let's write optimizer code carefully to avoid excessive Python overhead. Use local variables for speed.

Pseudo code:
```
def _optimize(p0, limit, global_state, rng, stoch_frac=0.75, c_start=6.0):
    p = _normalize(p0.copy())
    B = _conv(p)
    M = float(B.max())
    if M < global_state['M']: update
    best_local = p.copy(); best_local_M=M
    no_best=0
    it=0
    bad_base=0.01
    c_par=c_start
    stoch=stoch_frac
    lam_base=0.8
    # maybe local best vs global
    while time.monotonic() < limit:
       it +=1
       # update global if M improved
       if M < global_state['M']*(1-1e-12): ... no_best=0
       if global_state['M'] <= target/(2*n)*(1-1e-12): break
       # top selection
       # if B length L all? 
       width = max(M*0.08, 1e-18)
       count = int(np.sum(B >= M - width))
       K = max(1, count)
       if K < minK: K = min(L, minK)
       if K > maxK: K = maxK
       if K == L:
           top_idx = np.arange(L) # maybe cache? creating each time. Could set top_idx=None for full.
           top_vals = B
       else:
           top_idx = np.argpartition(B, L-K)[L-K:]? argpartition returns unsorted. For K maybe large, need ensure.
           top_vals = B[top_idx]
       # weights
       vmax = float(top_vals.max())
       w = np.exp(c_par * (top_vals - vmax)/max(vmax,1e-300))
       wsum = w.sum()
       if wsum <= 0 or not finite: w = np.full_like(w, 1.0/len(w))
       else: w/=wsum
       # choose k or full
       if L > 1 and rng.random() < stoch and K > 1:
           k = int(top_idx[np.random.choice(K, p=w)]) # top_idx unsorted, okay
           g = _single_grad(p,k)
       else:
           wfull = np.zeros(L)
           if len(top_idx)<L: wfull[top_idx]=w
           else: wfull=w
           c = _grad_w(p,wfull)
           g = 2.0*c
       gmax = float(g.max()); gmin=float(g.min())
       if not finite or gmax <= gmin + 1e-18:
           p = _perturb(p, amp=min(1.0, 0.1+0.01*abs(no_best)?), rng)
           B=_conv(p); M=B.max()
           no_best += 1
           continue
       u=(g-gmin)/(gmax-gmin)
       logp=np.log(p)
       # line search
       # dynamically lam_base: if no_best high, allow larger
       lam0 = 0.7 + 0.3*min(1.0, no_best/500)  # up to 1.0
       lams = [lam0, lam0*0.6, lam0*0.36, lam0*0.216]
       accepted=False
       best_cand_p=None; best_cand_M=M
       current_base_M = M
       for lam in lams:
           lp = logp - lam*u
           lp_max = lp.max()
           if not finite lp_max: continue
           pnew = np.exp(lp - lp_max)
           s = pnew.sum()
           if s <= 0: continue
           pnew /= s
           # floor
           if np.any(pnew < floor): pnew = np.maximum(pnew, floor); pnew/=pnew.sum()
           Bnew = _conv(pnew)
           Mnew = float(Bnew.max())
           if Mnew < best_cand_M:
               best_cand_p=pnew; best_cand_M=Mnew; best_cand_B=Bnew
           if Mnew < current_base_M * (1 - 1e-12):
               p=pnew; B=Bnew; M=Mnew; accepted=True; break
       if not accepted:
           # accept best candidate if within anneal
           if best_cand_p is not None:
               bad = current_base_M * (bad_base + 0.04*min(1.0,no_best/1000))
               if best_cand_M <= current_base_M + bad or best_cand_M < global_state['M']*(1-1e-12) or no_best > 1000:
                   p=best_cand_p; B=best_cand_B; M=best_cand_M
           # else keep
       # update global if improved using M
       if M < global_state['M']*(1-1e-10):
           global_state['M']=M; global_state['p']=p.copy(); no_best=0
       else:
           no_best +=1
       # stagnation resets
       if no_best > 800:
           # perturb from global best
           amp = 0.2 + 0.1*(no_best%10)
           p = _perturb(global_state['p'], amp, rng)
           B = _conv(p); M=float(B.max())
           no_best=0
           # maybe set c_par lower/high
           c_par = c_start
           stoch = 0.8
       elif no_best > 400:
           c_par = min(50.0, c_par*1.1)
           stoch = min(0.95, stoch+0.05)
       else:
           # slowly increase c_par
           c_par = min(30.0, c_start + it*0.001)
```

Now, one important point: The goal is to minimize `max_b` over full convolution. Our single-lag gradient update for k uses `g_j=2p_{k-j}`. But for self term when k=2j, C_k includes p_j^2 not double? Wait derivative of C_k = ∑ p_i p_{k-i}. For k=2j, the term p_j^2 derivative is 2p_j. The formula 2p_{k-j}=2p_j is correct. For i≠j, derivative wrt p_i is 2p_{k-i} (one from term i,k-i, one from k-i,i). So g = 2p_{k-j} correct. Good.

```
import numpy as np
import time

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    try: budget_s = float(budget_s)
    except: budget_s = 1300.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget_s) or budget_s <= 0: budget_s=1300.0
    deadline = t_start + max(1.0, budget_s - 25.0)
    seed = int(seed) if ... else 42
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    target = 1.50274349
    # parent
    parent = None
    try:
       parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
       parent = None
    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
       parent = np.where(np.isfinite(parent), parent, 0.0)
       parent = np.clip(parent, 0.0, 1000.0)
       if parent.sum() <= 1e-12:
           parent = None
    if parent is None:
       n = 6962 if budget_s > 300 else 1024
       parent = None
    else:
       n = int(parent.size)
    if n < 1:
       return [1.0, 1.0]
    if n == 1:
       return [1.0]
    # Precompute
    L = 2*n - 1
    floor = 1e-15
    conv_fft = 1 << max(1, (L - 1).bit_length())
    grad_fft = 1 << max(1, (L + n - 1 - 1).bit_length())
    target_M = target/(2.0*n)

    def normalize(p): ...
    def convB(p): ...
    def grad_w(p,w): ...
    def single_grad(p,k): ...
    def random_smooth(scale, base=None): ...
    def add_candidate(p,M): ...
    state={'best_M':np.inf,'best_p':None,'cands':[]}
    # create starts
    starts=[]
    if parent is not None:
        ppar = normalize(parent.copy())
        # if ppar all one? add tiny noise
        starts.append(ppar)
        for amp in (0.05,0.15,0.4): starts.append(random_smooth(amp, ppar))
    for scale in (0.3, 0.6, 1.0): starts.append(random_smooth(scale, None))
    # maybe two trig starts
    for f,amp in [(1,0.7),(2,0.6)]:
       i=np.arange(n)
       th=amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*f*i/n + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
       p=normalize((ppar if parent is not None else None) ...?) if ppar use else exp(th)
       starts.append(p)
    # Evaluate starts
    for p in starts:
       p=normalize(p)
       if p is not None:
          B=convB(p); M=float(B.max()); add_candidate(p,M)
    if state['best_p'] is None:
       p0=np.ones(n)/n; add_candidate(p0, 1/n? M from conv) return? 
    best_init = state['best_p'].copy()

    def run_optimize(p0, limit, stoch_frac=0.8): ...
    
    total = max(1.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    main_limit = t_start + total*0.62
    # ensure at least some time
    if main_limit < t_start+5: main_limit=deadline-1
    run_optimize(best_init, main_limit, 0.8)
    # perturb phase
    remain = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remain > 30 and state['best_p'] is not None:
        base=state['best_p']
        perturb_starts=[]
        for amp in (0.15, 0.4, 0.8): perturb_starts.append(random_smooth(amp, base.copy()))
        # maybe one random new
        perturb_starts.append(random_smooth(0.7, None))
        # sort by their M?
        ps=[]
        for p in perturb_starts:
           B=convB(p); M=float(B.max()); ps.append((M,p)); add_candidate(p,M)
        ps.sort()
        # allocate remaining split among up to 3
        k=min(3,len(ps))
        if k>0:
           # give first 1/3, second 1/3, third rest? Use equal with headroom
           ends = []
           start_time=time.monotonic()
           # We can create limits: main 2 runs 0.35 remain, third 0.28
           for i in range(k):
               frac = 0.35 if i<2 else 0.28
               lim = min(deadline - 5, time.monotonic() + remain*frac)
               if lim < time.monotonic()+5: break
               run_optimize(ps[i][1], lim, 1.0)  # stochastic polish
    # final exact
    candidates = state['cands'][:6]
    best_score=np.inf; best_seq=None
    # also include state best
    for p in candidates:
       if p is None: continue
       p = normalize(p)
       sc = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in p]))
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
          best_score=sc; best_seq=p
    if best_seq is None: fallback ones normalize
    # If exact didn't verify? return
    return [float(max(0.0,min(1000.0,x))) for x in best_seq]
```

Now, implement `add_candidate`:
```
def add_candidate(p, M):
    p = normalize(p)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(p)): return
    M = float(M)
    if not np.isfinite(M): return
    cands = state['cands']
    # Avoid too many similar? no
    if len(cands) < 8 or M < state['best_M']:
        cands.append((M, p.copy()))
        cands.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
        if len(cands)>8: cands.pop()
        state['best_M']=cands[0][0]
        state['best_p']=cands[0][1]
```
But if many candidates with similar M and len<8, state best only best. Good.

Now, `run_optimize` uses state and can update cands. It should also have local best? It can use state best for reset. Use local p.
```
def run_optimize(p0, limit, stoch_frac):
   if limit <= time.monotonic(): return
   p = normalize(p0)
   B = convB(p); M=float(B.max())
   add_candidate(p,M)
   it=0
   no_best=0
   c_par=6.0
   bad_base=0.008
   minK = min(L, max(8, min(64, L//16)))
   maxK = min(L, max(128, L//8))
   # If L < minK? set L
   minK = min(minK, L); maxK=max(maxK,minK)
   while time.monotonic() < limit:
       it += 1
       if M < state['best_M']*(1-1e-10):
           add_candidate(p,M); no_best=0
       if state['best_M'] <= target_M*(1-1e-12):
           break
       # if time too tight
       if time.monotonic() > limit - 0.5:
           break
       # threshold
       if target_M < M:
           threshold = max(target_M, M*0.90)
       else:
           threshold = M*0.90
       # count with sum maybe expensive; use np.sum
       if threshold <= 0:
           K = L
       else:
           count = int(np.sum(B >= threshold))
           if count < 0: count=0
           K = max(count, minK)
           K = min(K, maxK, L)
       if K <= L:
           if K == L:
               top_vals = B
               top_l = L
               top_index = None
           else:
               if K <= 0: K=1
               # argpartition. For K maybe 1, okay.
               part = np.argpartition(B, L-K)
               top_idx = part[L-K:L]
               top_vals = B[top_idx]
               top_index = top_idx
       # Need top_vals finite; if not, fallback
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(top_vals)) or top_vals.size==0:
           p=random_smooth(0.3,None); B=convB(p); M=float(B.max()); no_best+=1; continue
       vmax = float(top_vals.max())
       if vmax <= 0 or not finite: ...
       z = c_par * (top_vals - vmax) / vmax
       # if cpar*z large negative underflow; okay. But if vmax small? M >0.
       z = np.clip(z, -60.0, 0.0)
       w = np.exp(z)
       ws = float(w.sum())
       if ws <= 0 or not finite:
           w = np.ones(top_vals.size)/top_vals.size
       else: w/=ws
       if (top_index is None or rng.random() < stoch_frac) and top_vals.size > 1:
           if top_index is None:
               # sample from full with weights w? w length L maybe large; use rng.choice
               k = int(np.random.choice(L, p=w)) # But np.random.choice with p length 14k each time may be slow. Instead sample from top? If K=L, maybe use searchsorted? Could just k = int(rng.integers(0,L)) unweighted? For full active, approximate uniform. Use weighted only if not too large? Since full rare. Use `k=int(np.random.choice(L, p=w))` maybe okay.
           else:
               k = int(top_index[int(np.random.choice(top_vals.size, p=w))])
           g = single_grad(p,k)
       else:
           if top_index is None:
               wfull = w
           else:
               wfull = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
               wfull[top_index] = w
           cvec = grad_w(p, wfull)
           g = 2.0*cvec
       gmin=float(g.min()); gmax=float(g.max())
       if not (np.isfinite(gmin) and np.isfinite(gmax)) or gmax <= gmin + 1e-18:
           p = random_smooth(min(1.0, 0.2+0.05*min(5,no_best/500)), p) # perturb current
           B = convB(p); M = float(B.max()); add_candidate(p,M); no_best += 1; continue
       u = (g - gmin) / (gmax - gmin)
       # line search
       lam0 = 0.7 + 0.3*min(1.0, no_best/800.0)
       lams = (lam0, lam0*0.5, lam0*0.25)
       logp = np.log(p)
       best_cand = None
       best_cand_M = M
       best_cand_B = None
       for lam in lams:
           lp = logp - lam * u
           lpmax = float(lp.max())
           if not np.isfinite(lpmax): continue
           pn = np.exp(lp - lpmax)
           pn = np.maximum(pn, floor)
           s = float(pn.sum())
           if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s): continue
           pn /= s
           Bn = convB(pn); Mn = float(Bn.max())
           if Mn < best_cand_M:
               best_cand = pn; best_cand_M = Mn; best_cand_B = Bn
       if best_cand is not None and best_cand_M < M * (1 - 1e-10):
           p = best_cand; B = best_cand_B; M = best_cand_M
       else:
           # accept if not much worse or exploring
           if best_cand is not None:
               bad = M * (bad_base + 0.05*min(1.0, no_best/1000.0))
               if best_cand_M <= M + bad or best_cand_M < state['best_M']*(1 - 1e-12) or no_best > 1200:
                   p = best_cand; B = best_cand_B; M = best_cand_M
           # else keep
       p = normalize(p)
       # if p changed, B may not correspond if normalize changed tiny; recompute? normalize barely changes due floor. For safety, if difference >? recompute.
       # We can set B = convB(p) every iteration? That adds cost. Maybe not. But if normalize after p candidate, p already normalized. In reject keep, p same. In accept, p normalized by candidate. Good.
       # But if candidate pn normalized, yes. If perturbation branch, B recomputed.
       if M < state['best_M']*(1 - 1e-10):
           add_candidate(p,M); no_best=0
       else:
           no_best += 1
       if no_best > 900:
           amp = min(1.5, 0.25 + 0.05*(no_best % 20))
           p = random_smooth(amp, state['best_p'].copy() if state['best_p'] is not None else p)
           B = convB(p); M = float(B.max())
           add_candidate(p,M)
           no_best = 0
           c_par = 6.0
       elif no_best > 400:
           c_par = min(60.0, c_par * 1.1)
           stoch_frac = min(0.95, stoch_frac + 0.02)
       else:
           c_par = min(40.0, 6.0 + it*0.002)
```

```
def random_smooth(amp, base=None):
```

`normalize` uses n? It operates on input length. But all sequences length n. If parent capped, n updated. Good.
```
def normalize(p):
   p = np.array(p, dtype=np.float64, copy=True).ravel()
   if p.size != n:
      if p.size > n: p = p[:n]
      else: p = np.pad(p, (0,n-p.size), constant_values=1.0/n)
   p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
   p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
   s = float(p.sum())
   if s < 1e-9:
      p.fill(1.0/n)
      s=1.0
   else:
      p *= 1.0/s
   p = np.maximum(p, floor)
   s2=float(p.sum())
   if s2 > 0: p *= 1.0/s2
   return p
```
If p length not n, pad with 1/n? If input size less, okay. If p.size>n, truncate. But all length n.

For n=6962, floor 1e-15. If p fill uniform 1/n, then max floor? okay.

```
def convB(p):
   P=np.fft.rfft(p, conv_fft)
   B=np.fft.irfft(P*P, conv_fft)[:L]
   B = np.maximum(B, 0.0)
   # renormalize tiny imaginary? not.
   return B
```

```
def grad_w(p,w):
   w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   if w.size != L: 
       # if wrong, fallback? 
       if w.size > L: w=w[:L]
       else: tmp=np.zeros(L); tmp[:w.size]=w; w=tmp
   w = np.maximum(w,0.0)
   s=w.sum()
   if s <= 0: return np.zeros(n)
   w *= 1.0/s
   w_rev = w[::-1]
   W = np.fft.rfft(w_rev, grad_fft)
   P = np.fft.rfft(p, grad_fft)
   c = np.fft.irfft(W*P, grad_fft)
   c = c[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
   c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
   return c
```

```
def single_grad(p,k):
   g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
   if k < 0: k=int(k)%L? but k int 0..L-1.
   lo = k - n + 1
   if lo < 0: lo=0
   hi = k
   if hi > n-1: hi=n-1
   if lo <= hi:
       g[lo:hi+1] = 2.0 * p[k-hi : k-lo+1][::-1]
   return g
```

```
def random_smooth(amp, base=None):
```

```
    B = convB(p); M = float(B.max())
    add_candidate(p,M)

    minK = min(L, max(8, min(64, max(1, L//16))))
    maxK = min(L, max(128, L//8))
    if maxK < minK: maxK = minK
    while time.monotonic() < limit:
       ...
```

```
import numpy as np
import time

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try: budget_s = float(budget_s)
    except: budget_s = 1300.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget_s) or budget_s <= 1.0: budget_s = 1300.0
    hard_end = t0 + budget_s
    deadline = t0 + max(1.0, budget_s - 25.0)
    if deadline > hard_end - 2.0: deadline = hard_end - 2.0
    if deadline < t0 + 0.1: deadline = t0 + 0.1
    try: seed int
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    ...
```
If budget_s small (e.g., 10), deadline=t0+1, hard_end-2 maybe t0+8? deadline min? max(1.0, -15)=1; deadline=t0+1 < hard_end-2. Good. If budget_s=1, hard_end-2=t0-1, deadline=t0+1 > hard_end? `normalize` copy. If p is scalar? no.

```
    def normalize(p):
        p = np.array(p, dtype=np.float64, copy=True).ravel()
        if p.size != n:
            q = np.full(n, 1.0/n, dtype=np.float64)
            m = min(p.size, n)
            if m > 0:
                q[:m] = p[:m]
            p = q
        p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(p.sum())
        if s < 1e-9:
            p.fill(1.0 / n)
            return p
        p *= (1.0 / s)
        p = np.maximum(p, floor)
        s2 = float(p.sum())
        if s2 > 0.0:
            p *= (1.0 / s2)
        return p
```
If p uniform and then max floor, sum changes tiny; normalize again but only once. Fine.

`convB`:
```
    def convB(p):
        P = np.fft.rfft(p, conv_fft)
        B = np.fft.irfft(P * P, conv_fft)
        B = B[:L]
        B = np.maximum(B, 0.0)
        return B
```

`grad_w`:
```
    def grad_w(p, w):
        w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if w.size != L:
            tmp = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            m = min(w.size, L)
            if m: tmp[:m] = w[:m]
            w = tmp
        w = np.maximum(w, 0.0)
        s = float(w.sum())
        if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
            return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        if s != 1.0:
            w = w / s
        wr = w[::-1]
        W = np.fft.rfft(wr, grad_fft)
        P = np.fft.rfft(p, grad_fft)
        c = np.fft.irfft(W * P, grad_fft)
        c = c[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        return c
```

`single_grad` as above.

`random_smooth`:
```
    def random_smooth(amp, base=None):
        theta = rng.standard_normal(n)
        th = np.fft.rfft(theta)
        cut = max(2, th.size // 4)
        if cut < th.size:
            th[cut:] = 0.0
        theta = np.fft.irfft(th, n)
        theta -= theta.mean()
        if base is None:
            p = np.exp(amp * theta)
        else:
            p = np.asarray(base, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if p.size != n:
                p = normalize(p)
            else:
                p = p.copy()
            p = p * np.exp(amp * theta)
        return normalize(p)
```
If amp=0 and base not None, returns base normalized. Good.

`add_candidate`:
```
    def add_candidate(p, M):
        p = normalize(p)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            return
        M = float(M)
        if not np.isfinite(M):
            return
        cands = state['cands']
        if len(cands) < 8 or M < state['best_M']:
            cands.append((M, p.copy()))
            cands.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
            if len(cands) > 8:
                cands.pop()
            state['best_M'] = cands[0][0]
            state['best_p'] = cands[0][1]
```

```
    starts=[]

        ppar = normalize(parent.copy())
        starts.append(ppar)

    # power starts
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for gamma in (-0.45, -0.25, -0.1, 0.0, 0.2, 0.5):
        if gamma == 0.0:
            pp = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            pp = (idx + 1.0) ** gamma
            pp = np.maximum(pp, 1e-300)

    # right singular maybe same reverse; skip? add one reverse for gamma -0.45
    starts.append(normalize(np.maximum((n - idx), 1.0) ** -0.45))

```

Now, let's final output. The final answer should be one fenced Python block. No extra text. Ensure code is syntactically valid.

Let's write it carefully now.
</think>

```python
import numpy as np
import time


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget_s = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget_s = 1300.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget_s) or budget_s <= 1.0:
        budget_s = 1300.0

    hard_end = t0 + budget_s
    deadline = t0 + max(1.0, budget_s - 25.0)
    if deadline > hard_end - 2.0:
        deadline = hard_end - 2.0
    if deadline < t0 + 0.1:
        deadline = t0 + 0.1

    try:
        seed = int(seed)
    except Exception:
        seed = 42
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    target = 1.50274349

    parent = None
    try:
        par = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if par.size > 0:
            parent = par
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        parent = np.where(np.isfinite(parent), parent, 0.0)
        parent = np.clip(parent, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if parent.size == 0 or parent.sum() <= 1e-12:
            parent = None

    if parent is None:
        n = 6962 if budget_s > 300 else 1024
    else:
        n = int(parent.size)
        if n > 12000:
            m = 12000
            blocks = np.array_split(parent, m)
            par2 = np.array(
                [float(np.mean(b)) if b.size else 0.0 for b in blocks],
                dtype=np.float64,
            )
            par2 = np.clip(par2, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if par2.size == m and par2.sum() > 1e-12:
                parent = par2
                n = m

    if n < 2:
        return [1.0]

    L = 2 * n - 1
    floor = 1e-15
    conv_fft = 1 << max(1, (L - 1).bit_length())
    grad_fft = 1 << max(1, (L + n - 2).bit_length())
    target_M = target / (2.0 * n)

    state = {"best_M": np.inf, "best_p": None, "cands": []}

    def normalize(p):
        p = np.array(p, dtype=np.float64, copy=True).ravel()
        if p.size != n:
            q = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
            m = min(p.size, n)
            if m > 0:
                q[:m] = p[:m]
            p = q
        p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(p.sum())
        if s < 1e-9:
            p.fill(1.0 / n)
            return p
        p *= 1.0 / s
        p = np.maximum(p, floor)
        s2 = float(p.sum())
        if s2 > 0.0:
            p *= 1.0 / s2
        return p

    def convB(p):
        P = np.fft.rfft(p, conv_fft)
        B = np.fft.irfft(P * P, conv_fft)
        B = B[:L]
        B = np.maximum(B, 0.0)
        return B

    def grad_weighted(p, w):
        w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if w.size != L:
            tmp = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            m = min(w.size, L)
            if m > 0:
                tmp[:m] = w[:m]
            w = tmp
        w = np.maximum(w, 0.0)
        s = float(w.sum())
        if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
            return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        if s != 1.0:
            w = w / s
        wr = w[::-1]
        W = np.fft.rfft(wr, grad_fft)
        P = np.fft.rfft(p, grad_fft)
        c = np.fft.irfft(W * P, grad_fft)
        c = c[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        return c

    def single_grad(p, k):
        g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        k = int(k)
        lo = k - n + 1
        if lo < 0:
            lo = 0
        hi = k
        if hi > n - 1:
            hi = n - 1
        if lo <= hi:
            g[lo:hi + 1] = 2.0 * p[k - hi:k - lo + 1][::-1]
        return g

    def random_smooth(amp, base=None):
        theta = rng.standard_normal(n)
        th = np.fft.rfft(theta)
        cut = max(2, th.size // 4)
        if cut < th.size:
            th[cut:] = 0.0
        theta = np.fft.irfft(th, n)
        theta -= theta.mean()
        if base is None:
            p = np.exp(amp * theta)
        else:
            pb = np.asarray(base, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if pb.size != n:
                pb = normalize(pb)
            else:
                pb = pb.copy()
            p = pb * np.exp(amp * theta)
        return normalize(p)

    def add_candidate(p, M):
        p = normalize(p)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            return
        M = float(M)
        if not np.isfinite(M):
            return
        cands = state["cands"]
        if len(cands) < 8 or M < state["best_M"]:
            cands.append((M, p.copy()))
            cands.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
            if len(cands) > 8:
                cands.pop()
            state["best_M"] = cands[0][0]
            state["best_p"] = cands[0][1]

    starts = []
    if parent is not None:
        ppar = normalize(parent.copy())
        starts.append(ppar)
        for amp in (0.05, 0.15, 0.4):
            starts.append(random_smooth(amp, ppar.copy()))

    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for gamma in (-0.45, -0.25, -0.10, 0.0, 0.20, 0.50):
        if gamma == 0.0:
            pp = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            pp = (idx + 1.0) ** gamma
            pp = np.maximum(pp, 1e-300)
        starts.append(normalize(pp))
    starts.append(normalize(np.maximum(n - idx, 1.0) ** -0.45))
    for sc in (0.3, 0.7):
        starts.append(random_smooth(sc, None))

    for p in starts:
        try:
            p = normalize(p)
            B = convB(p)
            M = float(B.max())
            add_candidate(p, M)
        except Exception:
            pass

    if state["best_p"] is None:
        p0 = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
        B0 = convB(p0)
        add_candidate(p0, float(B0.max()))

    best_init = state["best_p"].copy()

    def run_optimize(p0, limit, stoch_frac):
        if limit <= time.monotonic() + 0.2:
            return

        p = normalize(p0)
        B = convB(p)
        M = float(B.max())
        add_candidate(p, M)

        no_best = 0
        it = 0
        c_par = 6.0
        bad_base = 0.008
        resets = 0

        minK = min(L, max(8, min(64, max(1, L // 16))))
        maxK = min(L, max(128, L // 8))
        if maxK < minK:
            maxK = minK

        while time.monotonic() < limit:
            it += 1
            if state["best_M"] <= target_M * (1.0 - 1e-12):
                break
            if time.monotonic() > limit - 0.5:
                break

            if not np.isfinite(M):
                p = random_smooth(0.5, None)
                B = convB(p)
                M = float(B.max())
                add_candidate(p, M)
                no_best += 1
                continue

            if M < state["best_M"] * (1.0 - 1e-10):
                add_candidate(p, M)
                no_best = 0

            if target_M < M:
                threshold = max(target_M, 0.90 * M)
            else:
                threshold = 0.90 * M

            if threshold <= 0.0:
                count = L
            else:
                count = int(np.sum(B >= threshold))
            K = max(count, minK)
            K = min(K, maxK, L)

            if K <= 0 or K >= L:
                top_vals = B
                top_index = None
            else:
                part = np.argpartition(B, L - K)
                top_index = part[L - K:]
                top_vals = B[top_index]

            if top_vals.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(top_vals)):
                p = random_smooth(0.35, None)
                B = convB(p)
                M = float(B.max())
                add_candidate(p, M)
                no_best += 1
                continue

            vmax = float(top_vals.max())
            if vmax <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(vmax):
                p = random_smooth(0.35, None)
                B = convB(p)
                M = float(B.max())
                add_candidate(p, M)
                no_best += 1
                continue

            z = c_par * (top_vals - vmax) / vmax
            z = np.clip(z, -60.0, 0.0)
            w = np.exp(z)
            ws = float(w.sum())
            if ws <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ws):
                w = np.ones(top_vals.size, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, top_vals.size)
            else:
                w /= ws

            if rng.random() < stoch_frac and top_vals.size > 1:
                if top_index is None:
                    if L <= 64:
                        order = np.argsort(B)[::-1]
                        pick = min(3, L)
                        k = int(order[int(rng.integers(0, pick))])
                    else:
                        k = int(np.argmax(B))
                else:
                    tv_size = top_vals.size
                    if rng.random() < 0.40:
                        k = int(top_index[int(np.argmax(top_vals))])
                    else:
                        k = int(top_index[int(rng.choice(tv_size, p=w))])
                g = single_grad(p, k)
            else:
                if top_index is None:
                    wfull = w
                else:
                    wfull = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
                    wfull[top_index] = w
                cvec = grad_weighted(p, wfull)
                g = 2.0 * cvec

            gmin = float(g.min())
            gmax = float(g.max())
            if not (np.isfinite(gmin) and np.isfinite(gmax)) or gmax <= gmin + 1e-18:
                amp = min(1.2, 0.20 + 0.03 * min(10, no_best // 100))
                p = random_smooth(amp, state["best_p"] if state["best_p"] is not None else p)
                B = convB(p)
                M = float(B.max())
                add_candidate(p, M)
                no_best += 1
                continue

            u = (g - gmin) / (gmax - gmin)
            logp = np.log(p)
            lam0 = 0.70 + 0.30 * min(1.0, no_best / 800.0)
            lams = (lam0, lam0 * 0.5, lam0 * 0.25)

            best_cand = None
            best_cand_M = M
            best_cand_B = None

            for lam in lams:
                lp = logp - lam * u
                lpmax = float(lp.max())
                if not np.isfinite(lpmax):
                    continue
                pn = np.exp(lp - lpmax)
                pn = np.maximum(pn, floor)
                s = float(pn.sum())
                if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
                    continue
                pn /= s
                Bn = convB(pn)
                Mn = float(Bn.max())
                if np.isfinite(Mn) and Mn < best_cand_M:
                    best_cand = pn
                    best_cand_M = Mn
                    best_cand_B = Bn

            if best_cand is not None and best_cand_M < M * (1.0 - 1e-10):
                p = best_cand
                B = best_cand_B
                M = best_cand_M
            elif best_cand is not None:
                bad = M * (bad_base + 0.05 * min(1.0, no_best / 1000.0))
                if (
                    best_cand_M <= M + bad
                    or best_cand_M < state["best_M"] * (1.0 - 1e-12)
                    or no_best > 1200
                ):
                    p = best_cand
                    B = best_cand_B
                    M = best_cand_M

            if M < state["best_M"] * (1.0 - 1e-10):
                add_candidate(p, M)
                no_best = 0
            else:
                no_best += 1

            if no_best > 800:
                resets += 1
                amp = min(1.5, 0.20 + 0.08 * (resets % 5))
                if resets % 4 == 0:
                    base = None
                else:
                    base = state["best_p"] if state["best_p"] is not None else p
                p = random_smooth(amp, base)
                B = convB(p)
                M = float(B.max())
                add_candidate(p, M)
                no_best = 0
                c_par = 6.0
            elif no_best > 400:
                c_par = min(60.0, c_par * 1.1)
                stoch_frac = min(0.95, stoch_frac + 0.02)
            else:
                c_par = min(40.0, 6.0 + 0.002 * it)

    total = max(1.0, deadline - t0)
    main_limit = t0 + total * 0.60
    if main_limit < t0 + 5.0:
        main_limit = deadline - 2.0
    if main_limit > deadline - 5.0:
        main_limit = deadline - 5.0
    run_optimize(best_init, main_limit, 0.85)

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 12.0 and state["best_p"] is not None:
        cand_starts = []

        def _eval_start(p):
            try:
                p = normalize(p)
                B = convB(p)
                M = float(B.max())
                add_candidate(p, M)
                cand_starts.append((M, p))
            except Exception:
                pass

        for amp in (0.08, 0.25, 0.60, 1.00):
            _eval_start(random_smooth(amp, state["best_p"]))
        for sc in (0.25, 0.75):
            _eval_start(random_smooth(sc, None))

        if cand_starts:
            cand_starts.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
            k = min(3, len(cand_starts))
            start_phase = time.monotonic()
            phase_end = deadline - 4.0
            rem_phase = phase_end - start_phase
            if rem_phase > 15.0:
                for i in range(k):
                    lim = start_phase + rem_phase * (i + 1) / float(k)
                    if lim > deadline - 3.0:
                        lim = deadline - 3.0
                    if lim > time.monotonic() + 3.0:
                        run_optimize(cand_starts[i][1], lim, 1.0)

    cands = state["cands"]
    if not cands:
        fallback = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
        cands = [(np.inf, fallback)]

    best_score = np.inf
    best_seq = None
    checked = 0

    for _, p in cands:
        if checked >= 8 or time.monotonic() > deadline - 1.0:
            break
        checked += 1
        try:
            p = normalize(p)
            sc = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in p]))
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_seq = p.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass

    if best_seq is None:
        if parent is not None:
            best_seq = normalize(parent)
        else:
            best_seq = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
        try:
            best_score = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in best_seq]))
        except Exception:
            best_score = np.inf
        if not np.isfinite(best_score):
            best_seq = np.ones(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64) / max(1, n)

    best_seq = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    best_seq = np.where(np.isfinite(best_seq), best_seq, 0.0)
    best_seq = np.clip(best_seq, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(best_seq.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        best_seq = np.ones(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64) / max(1, n)
    else:
        best_seq *= 1.0 / s
        if best_seq.size and float(best_seq.max()) > 1000.0:
            best_seq = np.clip(best_seq, 0.0, 1000.0)
            s2 = float(best_seq.sum())
            if s2 > 0.0:
                best_seq *= 1.0 / s2

    return [float(x) for x in best_seq]
```
